1. Context: what we are doing

We are working on the KANDA / PAULO project startup-delivery system.

The project root is assumed to be:

E:\kanda_reasoner

The generated “Show Project to AI” startup delivery folder is:

E:\kanda_reasoner_show_project_to_AI\first_prompt_files

The key generated startup files are:

first_prompts_to_ai.zip
prompt_library.zip
tell_AI_read_before_all.md
zz_read_only_if_modifying_startup_delivery.md

The generator/owner for these files is:

E:\kanda_reasoner\kanda_prompt_workspace\prompt_tools\sync_startup_routing_kernel_pack.py

The user’s requested behavior is:

Every time the startup delivery is generated, the AI must be instructed to read tell_AI_read_before_all.md first, before opening ZIP contents.

The correct intended startup reading order is:

1. tell_AI_read_before_all.md
2. first_prompts_to_ai.zip
3. prompt_library.zip
4. zz_read_only_if_modifying_startup_delivery.md only if modifying startup delivery

Important clarification from the user:

zz_read_only_if_modifying_startup_delivery.md is optional.
It must not be read during normal startup.
It is read only when modifying startup delivery.
If it is missing, pass and continue normal startup.

The read-order guard should appear:

- At the top of tell_AI_read_before_all.md.
- As the first file inside first_prompts_to_ai.zip:
  000_READ_TELL_AI_READ_BEFORE_ALL_FIRST.md
- As the first file inside prompt_library.zip:
  000_READ_TELL_AI_READ_BEFORE_ALL_FIRST.md
- At the top of zz_read_only_if_modifying_startup_delivery.md when that maintenance file is generated.

The generated guard text must clearly say:

Before any ZIP contents, first read tell_AI_read_before_all.md.

And it must explicitly say:

zz_read_only_if_modifying_startup_delivery.md - optional. Read only when modifying startup delivery. If this file is missing, pass and continue normal startup.

The uploaded refreshed startup files already show the desired text in tell_AI_read_before_all.md. It begins with:

# STARTUP DELIVERY READ ORDER - READ tell_AI_read_before_all.md FIRST

and lists the intended order with the optional/pass rule for zz_read_only_if_modifying_startup_delivery.md.

The broader implementation goal is to make this behavior canonical at the generator level, not by manually editing generated artifacts once. Future runs of sync_startup_routing_kernel_pack.py must reproduce the rule automatically.
2. What we have done so far
A. Startup pack was reloaded successfully

The user uploaded refreshed startup files:

first_prompts_to_ai.zip
prompt_library.zip
tell_AI_read_before_all.md

The read-order guard was followed in this chat:

1. tell_AI_read_before_all.md was read first.
2. first_prompts_to_ai.zip was opened after that.
3. prompt_library.zip was kept available but not fully opened unless a routed prompt is needed.

The startup load check was returned as COMPLETE / WAIT_FOR_TASK.

The refreshed tell_AI_read_before_all.md confirms the correct rule:

1. tell_AI_read_before_all.md - read this file first, before any ZIP contents.
2. first_prompts_to_ai.zip - after step 1, open this ZIP and read 00_START_HERE_FOR_AI.md first, then the numbered startup files in order.
3. prompt_library.zip - keep available, but open it only when startup routing selects a specific prompt_path that is not already inside first_prompts_to_ai.zip.
4. zz_read_only_if_modifying_startup_delivery.md - optional. Read only when modifying startup delivery. If this file is missing, pass and continue normal startup.

B. The original requested patch evolved through many versions

The patch series is:

C. Confirmed failures in the chain

The error chain started before v9. These are the known failures:
Error 1 — v1 old ZIP missing new notice caused KeyError

v1 made the generator/validator expect this file inside an existing old ZIP:

000_READ_TELL_AI_READ_BEFORE_ALL_FIRST.md

But older generated ZIPs did not have it yet. Validation crashed with:

KeyError: "There is no item named '000_READ_TELL_AI_READ_BEFORE_ALL_FIRST.md' in the archive"

Correct behavior:

If an old generated ZIP is missing the new notice file, treat it as stale/out-of-sync and regenerate it.
Do not crash with KeyError before regeneration.

Error 2 — v3/v4 optional maintenance-file wording not exact

v3/v4 failed because tell_AI_read_before_all.md did not contain the exact optional/pass rule.

The validation failures included:

Maintenance file is not marked optional in tell_AI_read_before_all.md.

and:

Maintenance optional/pass step is not exact in tell_AI_read_before_all.md.

Correct behavior:

tell_AI_read_before_all.md must explicitly say:
zz_read_only_if_modifying_startup_delivery.md is optional.
Read only when modifying startup delivery.
If missing, pass and continue normal startup.

Error 3 — v5/v6 generated helper had dangling raw-string assignment

v5 and v6 both failed during install with:

CANONICAL_HELPERS = r
NameError: name 'r' is not defined

This was a repeat error. The claimed fix did not actually inspect/compile the exact packaged helper inside the ZIP before delivery.

Correct behavior:

Before delivery, open the final ZIP payload.
Extract/read apply_*.py.
Assert it does not contain CANONICAL_HELPERS = r.
Run py_compile on the exact packaged helper.
Only then provide install instructions.

Error 4 — v7 corrupted sync_startup_routing_kernel_pack.py around content.find

v7 got past the prior helper error, but validation found that it wrote invalid Python into the generator:

SyntaxError: unterminated string literal
sync_startup_routing_kernel_pack.py line 2172
after = content.find("

Correct behavior:

If a patch helper modifies Python source, it must write a candidate file first.
Compile the candidate.
Only replace the real file if the candidate compiles.
Then compile the installed target before printing APPLY OK.

Error 5 — v8 corrupted sync_startup_routing_kernel_pack.py around re.finditer

v8 still corrupted the generator, this time around a regex string:

SyntaxError: unterminated string literal
sync_startup_routing_kernel_pack.py line 2177
matches = list(re.finditer(r"

Correct behavior:

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"

$PROJECT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner"
$PATCH_NAME = "kanda_startup_creator_canonical_zz_maintenance_v11_patch.zip"

function Invoke-PythonChecked {
    param(
        [string[]]$PythonArgs,
        [string]$Label,
        [string]$WorkDir
    )

    $stdoutFile = Join-Path $WorkDir ($Label + "_stdout.txt")
    $stderrFile = Join-Path $WorkDir ($Label + "_stderr.txt")

    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $stdoutFile -PathType Leaf) {
        Remove-Item -LiteralPath $stdoutFile -Force
    }
    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $stderrFile -PathType Leaf) {
        Remove-Item -LiteralPath $stderrFile -Force
    }

    $oldPreference = $ErrorActionPreference
    $ErrorActionPreference = "Continue"

    & python @PythonArgs 1> $stdoutFile 2> $stderrFile
    $exitCode = $LASTEXITCODE

    $ErrorActionPreference = $oldPreference

    $stdoutText = ""
    $stderrText = ""

    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $stdoutFile -PathType Leaf) {
        $stdoutText = Get-Content -LiteralPath $stdoutFile -Raw -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    }
    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $stderrFile -PathType Leaf) {
        $stderrText = Get-Content -LiteralPath $stderrFile -Raw -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    }

    if (-not [string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($stdoutText)) {
        Write-Host "----- $Label STDOUT -----"
        Write-Host $stdoutText
    }

    if (-not [string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($stderrText)) {
        Write-Host "----- $Label STDERR -----"
        Write-Host $stderrText
    }

    if ($exitCode -ne 0) {
        throw "$Label failed with code $exitCode. Stdout: $stdoutFile Stderr: $stderrFile"
    }

    return ($stdoutText + "`n" + $stderrText)
}

try {
    if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $PROJECT_ROOT -PathType Container)) {
        throw "Project root not found: $PROJECT_ROOT"
    }

    $PROJECT_NAME = Split-Path -Leaf $PROJECT_ROOT
    $DRIVE_ROOT = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($PROJECT_ROOT)
    $WORK_DIR = Join-Path $DRIVE_ROOT ($PROJECT_NAME + "_delete_after_daily_work")
    $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP = Join-Path $DRIVE_ROOT $PATCH_NAME
    $WORK_PATCH_ZIP = Join-Path $WORK_DIR $PATCH_NAME
    $EXTRACT_DIR = Join-Path $WORK_DIR "startup_creator_canonical_zz_v11_extract"
    $BACKUP_DIR = Join-Path $WORK_DIR ("startup_creator_canonical_zz_v11_backup_" + (Get-Date -Format "yyyyMMdd_HHmmss"))

    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $WORK_DIR | Out-Null

    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP -PathType Leaf) {
        Copy-Item -LiteralPath $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP -Destination $WORK_PATCH_ZIP -Force
        Remove-Item -LiteralPath $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP -Force
    }
    elseif (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $WORK_PATCH_ZIP -PathType Leaf)) {
        throw "Patch ZIP not found at drive root or staged work folder. Checked: $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP and $WORK_PATCH_ZIP"
    }

    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $EXTRACT_DIR) {
        Remove-Item -LiteralPath $EXTRACT_DIR -Recurse -Force
    }

    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $EXTRACT_DIR | Out-Null
    Expand-Archive -LiteralPath $WORK_PATCH_ZIP -DestinationPath $EXTRACT_DIR -Force

    $PAYLOAD_DIR = Join-Path $EXTRACT_DIR "payload"
    if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $PAYLOAD_DIR -PathType Container)) {
        throw "Payload folder missing after extraction: $PAYLOAD_DIR"
    }

    $VALIDATION_REL = "validation\test_startup_creator_canonical_zz_maintenance_v11.py"
    $VALIDATION_SRC = Join-Path $PAYLOAD_DIR $VALIDATION_REL
    $VALIDATION_DST = Join-Path $PROJECT_ROOT $VALIDATION_REL

    if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $VALIDATION_SRC -PathType Leaf)) {
        throw "Validation file missing from payload: $VALIDATION_SRC"
    }

    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $VALIDATION_DST -PathType Leaf) {
        $BACKUP_FILE = Join-Path $BACKUP_DIR $VALIDATION_REL
        $BACKUP_PARENT = Split-Path -Parent $BACKUP_FILE
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $BACKUP_PARENT | Out-Null
        Copy-Item -LiteralPath $VALIDATION_DST -Destination $BACKUP_FILE -Force
    }

    $VALIDATION_PARENT = Split-Path -Parent $VALIDATION_DST
    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $VALIDATION_PARENT | Out-Null
    Copy-Item -LiteralPath $VALIDATION_SRC -Destination $VALIDATION_DST -Force

    $APPLY_SCRIPT = Join-Path $PAYLOAD_DIR "patch_apply\apply_startup_creator_canonical_zz_maintenance_v11.py"

    if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $APPLY_SCRIPT -PathType Leaf)) {
        throw "Apply script missing from payload: $APPLY_SCRIPT"
    }

    Invoke-PythonChecked -PythonArgs @("-m", "py_compile", $APPLY_SCRIPT) -Label "apply_py_compile" -WorkDir $WORK_DIR

    $APPLY_OUTPUT = Invoke-PythonChecked -PythonArgs @($APPLY_SCRIPT, $PROJECT_ROOT) -Label "apply_script" -WorkDir $WORK_DIR

    if ($APPLY_OUTPUT -notmatch "APPLY OK: startup creator canonical zz maintenance v11") {
        throw "Apply script success marker was not found"
    }

    $SHOW_PROJECT_ROOT = Join-Path $DRIVE_ROOT ($PROJECT_NAME + "_show_project_to_AI")
    $ERROR_MEMORY_ROOT = Join-Path $SHOW_PROJECT_ROOT "project_error_memory"
    $PENDING_INTAKE_DIR = Join-Path $ERROR_MEMORY_ROOT "pending_ai_assisted_error_lesson_intake"

    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $PENDING_INTAKE_DIR | Out-Null

    $ERROR_BLOCK_DIR = Join-Path $PAYLOAD_DIR "error_memory_receive_blocks"
    if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $ERROR_BLOCK_DIR -PathType Container)) {
        throw "Error Memory receive block folder missing: $ERROR_BLOCK_DIR"
    }

    $ERROR_BLOCKS = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $ERROR_BLOCK_DIR -Filter "*.txt" -File

    if ($ERROR_BLOCKS.Count -lt 1) {
        throw "No Error Memory receive block files found in payload."
    }

    foreach ($BLOCK in $ERROR_BLOCKS) {
        $DST = Join-Path $PENDING_INTAKE_DIR $BLOCK.Name
        Copy-Item -LiteralPath $BLOCK.FullName -Destination $DST -Force

        if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $DST -PathType Leaf)) {
            throw "Error Memory receive block was not staged: $DST"
        }
    }

    Write-Host "INSTALL OK. Terminal will clear in 5 seconds..."
    Start-Sleep -Seconds 5
    Clear-Host
}
catch {
    Write-Host "INSTALL ERROR: $($_.Exception.Message)"
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "Diagnostic files are in: $WORK_DIR"
    Write-Host ""
    Read-Host "Press Enter to clear terminal"
    Clear-Host

    Read-Host "Press Enter again to finish"
    Clear-Host
}

in New local Freeze Entry  floating window, we need, right to button  "Confirm and write Freeze Entry" a button  "Ignore this Freeze"  that dont insert the current entry in cannon and forgets it.
in "Freeze feature after update" tab we need a button  "Get Last  Freeze" that copy the last freezed snipped  code for user give to AI (generalize used after updating freeze in "new local freeze Entry" floating window), and button "Get All Frozen"  both buttons right to  helsp button

This is a governed GUI/freezing workflow change, so I’ll treat it as a patch request. I’ll target the freeze tab and local freeze-entry dialog logic, while preserving the protected Confirm and Write gate.

Implemented as v12.

This patch updates the Freeze Feature After Update GUI/controller logic, not just generated files.

Patch ZIP:

What it changes:

- Adds "Ignore this Freeze" beside "Confirm and Write Freeze Entry" in New Local Freeze Entry.
- Ignore this Freeze does NOT write frozen memory.
- Ignore this Freeze clears/discards the current draft and marks the current freeze hint as ignored/used so it does not keep refilling the same draft.
- Adds "Get Last Freeze" and "Get All Frozen" beside Help in the Freeze Feature After Update tab.
- These buttons copy AI-ready frozen-memory snippets to clipboard.
- Adds Project Root label/path/Search controls compatible with the same outer-shell placement pattern used by Error Memory.
- Preserves Confirm and Write as the only write path for frozen memory.
